Le 17 octobre était un vendredi en 2025, donc cet article ne concerne pas octobre 2026.\n\nPour obtenir le bon match, je vous conseille de vérifier le calendrier officiel de la saison 2026-2027 sur le site de la LFP (ligue1.com), puis de chercher les pronostics de ce match précis, par exemple avec le nom de l'adversaire et la date. According to the Daily Courier, Cannan accused Dyas of backtracking on promises he made four years ago, while Dyas suggested Cannan has opposed major city projects that have benefited Kelowna. Homelessness, tied to social disorder and property crime, was one of the dominating topics. On that issue, Dyas said that if he succeeds in his lobbying, he would look to reduce municipal spending on crime and safety, calling the issue something that has been \"downloaded\" onto municipalities.\n\n- **Cannan's platform:** Cannan is running as a change candidate. He would make a core review of city operations one of his first priorities, examining programs, staffing, procurement and contracts for savings. He also criticized the current council for not pushing back hard enough on provincial housing and infrastructure requirements.\n\n- **Election date:** Voters will choose the next mayor, city council and school trustees on Oct. 17.\n\nI found no results about the forum's outcome or any polling, and I didn't search for any later debates.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"092e4978-c11f-4de4-b1f4-70c08e983c6c","uuid":"2b0123c5-a6a8-4450-a59e-e9806c9be464","timestamp":"2026-10-11T06:30:11.833Z","tool_use_result":{"query":"Kelowna mayoral race Cannan Dyas October 2026 castanet forum debate","results":[{"tool_use_id":"srvtoolu_017HegFqgY8pCYFUG2JFnEMA","content":[{"title":"Kelowna mayors debate at forum as municipal election draws near","url":"https://westknews.com/2026/10/08/kelowna-mayors-debate-at-forum-as-municipal-election-draws-near/"},{"title":"Kelowna’s Two Choices for Mayor","url":"https://queergranddad.substack.com/p/kelownas-two-choices-for-mayor"},{"title":"Dyas defends record while Cannan calls for change","url":"https://www.kelownadailycourier.ca/news/article_32d2c4d4-000e-4906-9afc-560f3e198f0a.html"},{"title":"Kelowna Chamber of Commerce hosts mayoral debate -
